   308

    24	async function getPendingCompensatoryHours(userId: string, excludeId?: string) {
    25	  const applications = await prisma.leaveApplication.findMany({
    26	    where: {
    27	      userId,
    28	      type: 'COMPENSATORY',
    29	      status: 'PENDING',
    30	      id: excludeId
    31	        ? {
    32	            not: excludeId,
    33	          }
    34	        : undefined,
    35	    },
    36	    select: {
    37	      days: true,
    38	    },
    39	  })
    40	
    41	  return applications.reduce((sum, application) => sum + application.days * SALARY_CONSTANTS.HOURS_PER_DAY, 0)
    42	}
    43	
    44	async function getHolidayDateBuckets(startDate: Date, endDate: Date) {
    45	  const holidays = await prisma.holiday.findMany({
    46	    where: {
    47	      date: {
    48	        gte: new Date(startDate.getFullYear(), startDate.getMonth(), startDate.getDate()),
    49	        lte: new Date(endDate.getFullYear(), endDate.getMonth(), endDate.getDate(), 23, 59, 59),
    50	      },
    51	    },
    52	    select: {
    53	      date: true,
    54	      type: true,
    55	    },
    56	  })
    57	
    58	  return {
    59	    legalHolidayDates: holidays
    60	      .filter((holiday) => holiday.type === 'LEGAL_HOLIDAY')
    61	      .map((holiday) => formatDateKey(new Date(holiday.date))),
    62	    compensatoryWorkDates: holidays
    63	      .filter((holiday) => holiday.type === 'COMPENSATORY')
    64	      .map((holiday) => formatDateKey(new Date(holiday.date))),
    65	  }
    66	}

    68	function buildLeavePayload(formData: FormData) {
    69	  const getString = (key: string) => {
    70	    const value = formData.get(key)
    71	    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined
    72	  }
    73	
    74	  const legacyHalfDaySession = getString('halfDaySession')
    75	  const validatedData = leaveSchema.parse({
    76	    type: getString('type'),
    77	    startSession: getString('startSession') ?? legacyHalfDaySession,
    78	    endSession: getString('endSession') ?? legacyHalfDaySession,
    79	    startDate: getString('startDate'),
    80	    endDate: getString('endDate'),
    81	    destination: getString('destination'),
    82	    reason: getString('reason'),
    83	  })
    84	
    85	  const startDateTime = new Date(validatedData.startDate)
    86	  const endDateTime = new Date(validatedData.endDate)
    87	  const startSession = validatedData.startSession || 'AM'
    88	  const endSession = validatedData.endSession || 'PM'
    89	
    90	  if (
    91	    formatDateKey(startDateTime) === formatDateKey(endDateTime) &&
    92	    startSession === 'PM' &&
    93	    endSession === 'AM'
    94	  ) {
    95	    throw new Error('同一天请假的结束时段不能早于开始时段')
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  return {
    99	    validatedData,
   100	    startDateTime,
   101	    endDateTime,
   102	    startSession,
   103	    endSession,
   104	  }
   105	}
   106	
   107	async function validateLeaveBalance(params: {
   108	  userId: string
   109	  leaveType: string
   110	  days: number
   111	  isCompensatory: boolean
   112	  excludeId?: string
   113	}) {
   114	  const balance = await ensureLeaveBalance(params.userId)
   115	
   116	  if (params.isCompensatory) {
   117	    const pendingCompensatoryHours = await getPendingCompensatoryHours(params.userId, params.excludeId)
   118	    const availableCompensatory =
   119	      (balance.compensatory || 0) - (balance.usedCompensatory || 0) - pendingCompensatoryHours
   120	
   121	    if (availableCompensatory < params.days * SALARY_CONSTANTS.HOURS_PER_DAY) {
   122	      return { error: `调休余额不足，当前可用 ${availableCompensatory} 小时` }
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    return { balance }
   126	  }
   127	
   128	  let currentBalance = 0
   129	  if (params.leaveType === 'ANNUAL') {
   130	    currentBalance = balance.annual
   131	  } else if (params.leaveType === 'SICK') {
   132	    currentBalance = balance.sick
   133	  } else if (params.leaveType === 'PERSONAL') {
   134	    currentBalance = balance.personal
   135	  }
   136	
   137	  if (
   138	    params.days > currentBalance &&
   139	    params.leaveType !== 'MARRIAGE' &&
   140	    params.leaveType !== 'MATERNITY' &&
   141	    params.leaveType !== 'PATERNITY'
   142	  ) {
   143	    return { error: `假期余额不足，当前剩余 ${currentBalance} 天` }
   144	  }
   145	
   146	  return { balance }
   147	}

   149	async function requireLeaveOwnerOrAdmin(id: string) {

   151	  const application = await prisma.leaveApplication.findUnique({

   157	      approverId: true,
   158	    },
   159	  })
   160	
   161	  if (!application) {
   162	    return {
   163	      sessionUser,
   164	      application: null,
   165	    }
   166	  }
   167	
   168	  if (sessionUser.role !== 'ADMIN' && sessionUser.id !== application.userId) {
   169	    throw new Error('无权操作该请假申请')
   170	  }
   171	
   172	  return {
   173	    sessionUser,
   174	    application,

   178	export async function getLeaveDurationPreview(
   179	  startDate?: string,
   180	  endDate?: string,
   181	  startSession?: string,
   182	  endSession?: string
   183	) {
   184	  try {
   185	    await requireSessionUser()
   186	    const normalizedStartSession = startSession === 'PM' ? 'PM' : 'AM'
   187	    const normalizedEndSession = endSession === 'AM' ? 'AM' : 'PM'
   188	
   189	    if (!startDate || !endDate) {
   190	      return { days: 0 }
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    const start = new Date(startDate)
   194	    const end = new Date(endDate)
   195	
   196	    if (Number.isNaN(start.getTime()) || Number.isNaN(end.getTime()) || end < start) {
   197	      return { days: 0 }
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    const holidayBuckets = await getHolidayDateBuckets(start, end)
   201	    const days = calculateLeaveDaysExcludingNonWorkingDays(start, end, {
   202	      ...holidayBuckets,
   203	      startSession: normalizedStartSession,
   204	      endSession: normalizedEndSession,
   205	    })
   206	
   207	    return { days }
   208	  } catch (error) {
   209	    console.error('获取请假天数预览失败:', error)
   210	    return { days: 0 }
   211	  }
   212	}

   214	export async function createLeaveApplication(formData: FormData) {
   215	  try {
   216	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()

   221	      ...holidayBuckets,
   222	      startSession: payload.startSession,
   223	      endSession: payload.endSession,
   224	    })
   225	
   226	    if (days <= 0) {
   227	      return { error: '所选日期不包含有效工作日，请重新选择' }

   229	
   230	    if (action === 'submit') {
   231	      const balanceCheck = await validateLeaveBalance({

   234	        days,
   235	        isCompensatory: payload.validatedData.type === 'COMPENSATORY',
   236	      })
   237	
   238	      if (balanceCheck.error) {
   239	        return { error: balanceCheck.error }
   240	      }
   241	    }
   242	
   243	    const created = await prisma.leaveApplication.create({
   244	      data: {
   245	        userId: sessionUser.id,
   246	        type: payload.validatedData.type,
   247	        startSession: payload.startSession || null,

   249	        halfDaySession: null,

   252	        days,
   253	        reason: payload.validatedData.reason,

   255	        status: action === 'submit' ? 'PENDING' : 'DRAFT',
   256	      },
   257	    })
   258	
   259	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/leave')
   260	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/compensatory')
   261	
   262	    const applicationLabel = payload.validatedData.type === 'COMPENSATORY' ? '调休申请' : '请假申请'
   263	
   264	    return {
   265	      success: action === 'submit' ? `${applicationLabel}已提交` : `${applicationLabel.replace('申请', '')}草稿已保存`,
   266	      id: created.id,
   267	      status: created.status,
   268	    }
   269	  } catch (error) {
   270	    if (error instanceof Error) {
   271	      return { error: error.message }
   272	    }
   273	    return { error: '提交失败，请稍后重试' }

   299	export async function deleteLeaveApplication(id: string) {
   300	  try {
   301	    if (!id) {
   302	      return { error: '缺少请假申请 ID' }
   303	    }
   304	
   305	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
   306	    const application = await prisma.leaveApplication.findUnique({
   307	      where: { id },
   308	      include: {
   309	        user: {
   310	          select: {
   311	            departmentId: true,
   312	          },
   313	        },
   314	      },
   315	    })
   316	
   317	    if (!application) {
   318	      return { error: '请假申请不存在' }
   319	    }
   320	
   321	    const isOwner = sessionUser.id === application.userId
   322	    const isAdmin = sessionUser.role === 'ADMIN'
   323	    const isClerkDeletingImport =
   324	      isAttendanceClerk(sessionUser.role) &&
   325	      application.status === 'COMPLETED' &&
   326	      application.approverId === null
   327	
   328	    if (!isOwner && !isAdmin && !isClerkDeletingImport) {
   329	      return { error: '无权操作该请假申请' }
   330	    }
   331	
   332	    if (application.status !== 'DRAFT' && !isAdmin && !isClerkDeletingImport) {
   333	      return { error: '只有草稿状态的请假申请可以删除' }
   334	    }
   335	
   336	    await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
   337	      // 如果是导入的已完成请假，删除时恢复余额
   338	      if (isClerkDeletingImport && ['ANNUAL', 'SICK', 'PERSONAL', 'COMPENSATORY'].includes(application.type)) {
   339	        const balance = await tx.leaveBalance.findFirst({
   340	          where: {
   341	            userId: application.userId,
   342	            year: new Date().getFullYear(),
   343	          },
   344	        })
   345	
   346	        if (balance) {
   347	          const updateData: Record<string, { increment: number } | { decrement: number }> = {}
   348	          if (application.type === 'ANNUAL') {
   349	            updateData.annual = { increment: application.days }
   350	          } else if (application.type === 'SICK') {
   351	            updateData.sick = { increment: application.days }
   352	          } else if (application.type === 'PERSONAL') {
   353	            updateData.personal = { increment: application.days }
   354	          } else if (application.type === 'COMPENSATORY') {
   355	            updateData.usedCompensatory = { decrement: application.days * SALARY_CONSTANTS.HOURS_PER_DAY }
   356	          }
   357	
   358	          if (Object.keys(updateData).length > 0) {
   359	            await tx.leaveBalance.update({
   360	              where: { id: balance.id },
   361	              data: updateData,
   362	            })
   363	          }
   364	        }
   365	      }
   366	
   367	      await tx.approval.deleteMany({
   368	        where: {
   369	          applicationId: id,
   370	          applicationType: 'LEAVE',
   371	        },
   372	      })
   373	      await tx.leaveApplication.delete({
   374	        where: { id },
   375	      })
   376	    })
   377	
   378	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/leave')
   379	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/compensatory')
   380	    return { success: '请假申请已删除' }
   381	  } catch (error) {
   382	    if (error instanceof Error) {
   383	      return { error: error.message }
   384	    }
   385	    return { error: '删除失败，请稍后重试' }
   386	  }
   387	}

   439	      }),

   466	    }

   475	export async function getLeaveApplications(_userId?: string, _role?: string) {
   476	  try {
   477	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()

   502	      startSession: application.startSession || application.halfDaySession || null,

   504	      halfDaySession: application.halfDaySession,

   506	        application.type === 'COMPENSATORY' ? application.days * SALARY_CONSTANTS.HOURS_PER_DAY : 0,
   507	    }))

   514	export async function getLeaveBalances(userId: string) {
   515	  try {
   516	    if (!userId) {
   517	      return null
   518	    }
   519	
   520	    await requireSelfOrAdmin(userId)
   521	
   522	    const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
   523	      where: { id: userId },

   525	    })
   526	
   527	    if (!user) {

   529	    }
   530	
   531	    return await ensureLeaveBalance(userId)
   532	  } catch (error) {
   533	    console.error('获取假期余额失败:', error)
   534	    return null
   535	  }
   536	}
   537	
   538	export async function getLeaveStats(userId?: string, departmentId?: string, referenceMonth?: string) {
   539	  try {
   540	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
   541	    const now = new Date()
   542	    const [year, month] = referenceMonth
   543	      ? referenceMonth.split('-').map(Number)
   544	      : [now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1]
   545	    const firstDayOfMonth = new Date(year, month - 1, 1)
   546	    const lastDayOfMonth = new Date(year, month, 0, 23, 59, 59)
   547	
   548	    const where: Prisma.LeaveApplicationWhereInput = {
   549	      status: {
   550	        in: ['APPROVED', 'COMPLETED'],
   551	      },
   552	      startDate: {
   553	        lte: lastDayOfMonth,
   554	      },
   555	      endDate: {
   556	        gte: firstDayOfMonth,
   557	      },
   558	    }
   559	
   560	    if (sessionUser.role === 'EMPLOYEE') {
   561	      where.userId = sessionUser.id
   562	    } else if (userId) {
   563	      where.userId = userId
   564	    }
   565	
   566	    if (departmentId && sessionUser.role !== 'EMPLOYEE') {
   567	      where.user = {
   568	        departmentId,
   569	      }
   570	    }
   571	
   572	    const applications = await prisma.leaveApplication.findMany({
   573	      where,
   574	      select: {
   575	        days: true,
   576	        startDate: true,
   577	        endDate: true,
   578	      },
   579	    })
   580	
   581	    let totalDays = 0
   582	    for (const application of applications) {
   583	      const leaveStart = new Date(application.startDate)
   584	      const leaveEnd = new Date(application.endDate)
   585	
   586	      const effectiveStart = leaveStart < firstDayOfMonth ? firstDayOfMonth : leaveStart
   587	      const effectiveEnd = leaveEnd > lastDayOfMonth ? lastDayOfMonth : leaveEnd
   588	
   589	      const msPerDay = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
   590	      const overlapDays = Math.ceil((effectiveEnd.getTime() - effectiveStart.getTime()) / msPerDay) + 1
   591	      const totalLeaveDays = (leaveEnd.getTime() - leaveStart.getTime()) / msPerDay + 1
   592	      const ratio = overlapDays / totalLeaveDays
   593	      totalDays += application.days * ratio
   594	    }
   595	
   596	    return Math.round(totalDays * 2) / 2
   597	  } catch (error) {
   598	    console.error('获取请假统计失败:', error)
   599	    return 0
   600	  }
   601	}
   602	
   603	
   604	// ===== 批量导入请假 =====
   605	
   606	export async function batchImportLeave(rows: ParsedLeaveRow[]) {
   607	  try {
   608	    await requireAttendanceClerk()
   609	
   610	    if (!rows || rows.length === 0) {
   611	      return { error: '没有数据需要导入' }
   612	    }
   613	
   614	    const usernames = Array.from(new Set(rows.map((r) => r.username)))
   615	    const users = await prisma.user.findMany({
   616	      where: {
   617	        username: {
   618	          in: usernames,
   619	        },
   620	      },
   621	      select: {
   622	        id: true,
   623	        username: true,
   624	        name: true,
   625	      },
   626	    })
   627	
   628	    const userMap = new Map(users.map((u) => [u.username, u]))
   629	    const errors: Array<{ rowIndex: number; message: string }> = []
   630	
   631	    for (const row of rows) {
   632	      const user = userMap.get(row.username)
   633	      if (!user) {
   634	        errors.push({ rowIndex: row.rowIndex, message: `用户 "${row.username}" 不存在` })
   635	      } else if (user.name !== row.name && row.name) {
   636	        errors.push({ rowIndex: row.rowIndex, message: `用户 "${row.username}" 的姓名不匹配（系统中为 ${user.name}）` })
   637	      }
   638	    }
   639	
   640	    if (errors.length > 0) {
   641	      return { error: '数据校验失败', errors }
   642	    }
   643	
   644	    const now = new Date()
   645	
   646	    await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
   647	      for (const row of rows) {
   648	        const user = userMap.get(row.username)!
   649	        const startDateTime = new Date(row.startDate)
   650	        const endDateTime = new Date(row.endDate)
   651	        const startSession = row.startSession || 'AM'
   652	        const endSession = row.endSession || 'PM'
   653	
   654	        if (
   655	          formatDateKey(startDateTime) === formatDateKey(endDateTime) &&
   656	          startSession === 'PM' &&
   657	          endSession === 'AM'
   658	        ) {
   659	          throw new Error(`第 ${row.rowIndex} 行：同一天请假的结束时段不能早于开始时段`)
   660	        }
   661	
   662	        const holidays = await tx.holiday.findMany({
   663	          where: {
   664	            date: {
   665	              gte: new Date(startDateTime.getFullYear(), startDateTime.getMonth(), startDateTime.getDate()),
   666	              lte: new Date(endDateTime.getFullYear(), endDateTime.getMonth(), endDateTime.getDate(), 23, 59, 59),
   667	            },
   668	          },
   669	          select: {
   670	            date: true,
   671	            type: true,
   672	          },
   673	        })
   674	
   675	        const legalHolidayDates = holidays
   676	          .filter((h) => h.type === 'LEGAL_HOLIDAY')
   677	          .map((h) => formatDateKey(new Date(h.date)))
   678	        const compensatoryWorkDates = holidays
   679	          .filter((h) => h.type === 'COMPENSATORY')
   680	          .map((h) => formatDateKey(new Date(h.date)))
   681	
   682	        const days = calculateLeaveDaysExcludingNonWorkingDays(startDateTime, endDateTime, {
   683	          legalHolidayDates,
   684	          compensatoryWorkDates,
   685	          startSession,
   686	          endSession,
   687	        })
   688	
   689	        if (days <= 0) {
   690	          throw new Error(`第 ${row.rowIndex} 行：所选日期不包含有效工作日`)
   691	        }
   692	
   693	        const leaveType = row.type
   694	        const isCompensatory = leaveType === 'COMPENSATORY'
   695	
   696	        const balance = await tx.leaveBalance.findFirst({
   697	          where: {
   698	            userId: user.id,
   699	            year: now.getFullYear(),
   700	          },
   701	        })
   702	
   703	        if (!balance) {
   704	          throw new Error(`第 ${row.rowIndex} 行：用户 ${row.username} 没有假期余额记录`)
   705	        }
   706	
   707	        if (isCompensatory) {
   708	          const availableCompensatory = (balance.compensatory || 0) - (balance.usedCompensatory || 0)
   709	          if (availableCompensatory < days * SALARY_CONSTANTS.HOURS_PER_DAY) {
   710	            throw new Error(`第 ${row.rowIndex} 行：用户 ${row.username} 调休余额不足，当前可用 ${availableCompensatory} 小时`)
   711	          }
   712	        } else if (
   713	          leaveType !== 'MARRIAGE' &&
   714	          leaveType !== 'MATERNITY' &&
   715	          leaveType !== 'PATERNITY'
   716	        ) {
   717	          let currentBalance = 0
   718	          if (leaveType === 'ANNUAL') currentBalance = balance.annual
   719	          else if (leaveType === 'SICK') currentBalance = balance.sick
   720	          else if (leaveType === 'PERSONAL') currentBalance = balance.personal
   721	
   722	          if (days > currentBalance) {
   723	            throw new Error(`第 ${row.rowIndex} 行：用户 ${row.username} 假期余额不足，当前剩余 ${currentBalance} 天`)
   724	          }
   725	        }
   726	
   727	        await tx.leaveApplication.create({
   728	          data: {
   729	            userId: user.id,
   730	            type: leaveType,
   731	            startSession: startSession || null,
   732	            endSession: endSession || null,
   733	            halfDaySession: null,
   734	            startDate: startDateTime,
   735	            endDate: endDateTime,
   736	            days,
   737	            reason: row.reason,
   738	            destination: row.destination || null,
   739	            status: 'COMPLETED',
   740	            approverId: null,
   741	            approvedAt: now,
   742	          },
   743	        })
   744	
   745	        const balanceUpdate: Record<string, { decrement: number } | { increment: number }> = {}
   746	        if (leaveType === 'ANNUAL') {
   747	          balanceUpdate.annual = { decrement: days }
   748	        } else if (leaveType === 'SICK') {
   749	          balanceUpdate.sick = { decrement: days }
   750	        } else if (leaveType === 'PERSONAL') {
   751	          balanceUpdate.personal = { decrement: days }
   752	        } else if (leaveType === 'COMPENSATORY') {
   753	          balanceUpdate.usedCompensatory = { increment: days * SALARY_CONSTANTS.HOURS_PER_DAY }
   754	        }
   755	
   756	        if (Object.keys(balanceUpdate).length > 0) {
   757	          await tx.leaveBalance.update({
   758	            where: { id: balance.id },
   759	            data: balanceUpdate,
   760	          })
   761	        }
   762	      }
   763	    })
   764	
   765	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/leave')
   766	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/compensatory')
   767	    return { success: `成功导入 ${rows.length} 条请假记录` }
   768	  } catch (error) {
   769	    if (error instanceof Error) {
   770	      return { error: error.message }
   771	    }
   772	    return { error: '导入失败，请稍后重试' }
   773	  }
   774	}


</details>

   314	        <CardHeader>
   315	          <CardTitle>
   316	            {activeTab === 'overtime' ? '加班记录' : '休假记录'}
   317	            <span className="ml-2 text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">
   318	              共 {activeTab === 'overtime' ? overtimeTotal : leaveTotal} 条
   319	            </span>
   320	          </CardTitle>
   321	        </CardHeader>
   322	        <CardContent>

   325	          ) : activeTab === 'overtime' ? (

   327	          ) : (

   330	        </CardContent>
   331	        <CardContent className="pt-0">
   332	          <PaginationControls
   333	            currentPage={pagination.currentPage}
   334	            itemLabel="条记录"
   335	            onPageChange={setCurrentPage}
   336	            onPageSizeChange={setPageSize}
   337	            pageSize={pageSize}
   338	            pageSizeOptions={PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS}
   339	            totalItems={activeTab === 'overtime' ? overtimeTotal : leaveTotal}
   340	            totalPages={pagination.totalPages}
   341	          />
   342	        </CardContent>
   343	      </Card>
   344	    </div>
   345	  )

   347	
   348	function OvertimeTable({ data }: { data: OvertimeItem[] }) {
   349	  if (data.length === 0) {
   350	    return (
   351	      <div className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">当前筛选条件下暂无加班记录</div>
   352	    )
   353	  }
   354	
   355	  return (
   356	    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
   357	      <Table>
   358	        <TableHeader>
   359	          <TableRow>
   360	            <TableHead>日期</TableHead>
   361	            <TableHead>申请人</TableHead>
   362	            <TableHead>部门</TableHead>
   363	            <TableHead>时间段</TableHead>
   364	            <TableHead>计划时长</TableHead>
   365	            <TableHead>实际时长</TableHead>
   366	            <TableHead>类型</TableHead>
   367	            <TableHead>事由</TableHead>
   368	            <TableHead>状态</TableHead>
   369	            <TableHead>备注</TableHead>
   370	          </TableRow>
   371	        </TableHeader>
   372	        <TableBody>
   373	          {data.map((item) => (
   374	            <TableRow key={item.id}>
   375	              <TableCell>{new Date(item.date).toLocaleDateString('zh-CN')}</TableCell>
   376	              <TableCell>{item.userName}</TableCell>
   377	              <TableCell>{item.departmentName || '-'}</TableCell>
   378	              <TableCell>
   379	                {new Date(item.startTime).toLocaleTimeString('zh-CN', {
   380	                  hour: '2-digit',
   381	                  minute: '2-digit',
   382	                })}{' '}
   383	                -{' '}
   384	                {new Date(item.endTime).toLocaleTimeString('zh-CN', {
   385	                  hour: '2-digit',
   386	                  minute: '2-digit',
   387	                })}
   388	              </TableCell>
   389	              <TableCell>{item.hours} 小时</TableCell>
   390	              <TableCell>
   391	                {item.actualHours ? `${item.actualHours} 小时` : '-'}
   392	              </TableCell>
   393	              <TableCell>
   394	                {item.type === 'WEEKEND' ? '周末' : item.type === 'HOLIDAY' ? '节假日' : '工作日'}
   395	              </TableCell>
   396	              <TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate">{item.reason}</TableCell>
   397	              <TableCell>
   398	                <Badge variant={statusVariantMap[item.status] || 'default'}>
   399	                  {item.statusText}
   400	                </Badge>
   401	              </TableCell>
   402	              <TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate">{item.remark || '-'}</TableCell>
   403	            </TableRow>
   404	          ))}
   405	        </TableBody>
   406	      </Table>
   407	    </div>
   408	  )
   409	}

    35	function buildTimeRangeWhere(range: TimeRange): { gte?: Date; lte?: Date } | undefined {
    36	  if (range === 'all') {
    37	    return undefined
    38	  }
    39	
    40	  const now = new Date()
    41	  const start = new Date(now)
    42	  start.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
    43	  start.setMonth(start.getMonth() - (range === 'last1Month' ? 1 : 3))
    44	
    45	  return {
    46	    gte: start,
    47	    lte: now,
    48	  }
    49	}

    99	function buildOvertimeWhere(filters: QueryFilters): Prisma.OvertimeApplicationWhereInput {
   100	  const where: Prisma.OvertimeApplicationWhereInput = {}
   101	
   102	  if (filters.departmentId) {
   103	    where.user = { ...(where.user as object), departmentId: filters.departmentId }
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  if (filters.userName) {
   107	    where.user = {
   108	      ...(where.user as object),
   109	      name: { contains: filters.userName, mode: 'insensitive' },
   110	    }
   111	  }
   112	
   113	  if (filters.status) {
   114	    where.status = filters.status
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  if (filters.type) {
   118	    where.type = filters.type
   119	  }
   120	
   121	  const dateRange = buildTimeRangeWhere(filters.timeRange || 'all')
   122	  if (dateRange) {
   123	    where.date = dateRange
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  return where
   127	}
   128	
   129	function buildLeaveWhere(filters: QueryFilters): Prisma.LeaveApplicationWhereInput {
   130	  const where: Prisma.LeaveApplicationWhereInput = {}
   131	
   132	  if (filters.departmentId) {
   133	    where.user = { ...(where.user as object), departmentId: filters.departmentId }
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  if (filters.userName) {
   137	    where.user = {
   138	      ...(where.user as object),
   139	      name: { contains: filters.userName, mode: 'insensitive' },
   140	    }
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  if (filters.status) {

   145	  }
   146	
   147	  if (filters.type) {
   148	    where.type = filters.type
   149	  }
   150	
   151	  const dateRange = buildTimeRangeWhere(filters.timeRange || 'all')
   152	  if (dateRange) {
   153	    where.startDate = { gte: dateRange.gte }
   154	    where.endDate = { lte: dateRange.lte }
   155	  }
   156	
   157	  return where
   158	}

   160	export async function getQueryOvertimeList(
   161	  filters: QueryFilters,
   162	  page: number,
   163	  pageSize: number
   164	) {
   165	  try {
   166	    await requireManagerUser()
   167	
   168	    const where = buildOvertimeWhere(filters)
   169	    const skip = (page - 1) * pageSize
   170	
   171	    const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
   172	      prisma.overtimeApplication.findMany({
   173	        where,
   174	        include: {
   175	          user: {
   176	            select: {
   177	              name: true,
   178	              department: {
   179	                select: { name: true },
   180	              },
   181	            },
   182	          },
   183	        },
   184	        orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
   185	        skip,
   186	        take: pageSize,
   187	      }),
   188	      prisma.overtimeApplication.count({ where }),
   189	    ])
   190	
   191	    const mappedItems: OvertimeQueryItem[] = items.map((item) => ({
   192	      id: item.id,
   193	      userId: item.userId,
   194	      userName: item.user.name,
   195	      departmentName: item.user.department?.name,
   196	      date: item.date,
   197	      startTime: item.startTime,
   198	      endTime: item.endTime,
   199	      hours: item.hours,
   200	      actualStartTime: item.actualStartTime,
   201	      actualEndTime: item.actualEndTime,
   202	      actualHours: item.actualHours,
   203	      type: item.type,
   204	      reason: item.reason,
   205	      status: item.status,
   206	      statusText: overtimeStatusMap[item.status] || item.status,
   207	      remark: item.remark,
   208	      createdAt: item.createdAt,
   209	    }))
   210	
   211	    return { items: mappedItems, total }
   212	  } catch (error) {
   213	    console.error('查询加班列表失败:', error)
   214	    return { items: [], total: 0 }
   215	  }
   216	}

    26	import { deleteOvertimeApplication, getOvertimeApplications } from '@/server/actions/overtime'
    27	
    28	type OvertimeApplicationItem = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getOvertimeApplications>>[number]
    29	
    30	const statusMap: Record<string, string> = {
    31	  DRAFT: '草稿',
    32	  PENDING: '事前审批中',
    33	  PRE_APPROVED: '事前通过待确认',
    34	  CONFIRM_PENDING: '确认审批中',
    35	  COMPLETED: '已完成',
    36	  REJECTED: '已退回',
    37	}
    38	
    39	const statusVariant: Record<string, 'default' | 'warning' | 'success' | 'danger' | 'info'> = {
    40	  DRAFT: 'default',
    41	  PENDING: 'warning',
    42	  PRE_APPROVED: 'info',
    43	  CONFIRM_PENDING: 'warning',
    44	  COMPLETED: 'success',
    45	  REJECTED: 'danger',
    46	}
    47	
    48	export default function OvertimePage() {
    49	  const { data: session } = useSession()
    50	  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<OvertimeApplicationItem[]>([])
    51	  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1)
    52	  const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE)
    53	  const [timeRange, setTimeRange] = useState<TimeRange>('all')
    54	
    55	  const role = session?.user?.role
    56	  const canCreate = !!session?.user?.id
    57	  const showApplicant = role !== 'EMPLOYEE'
    58	  const showActions = role === 'EMPLOYEE' || role === 'ATTENDANCE_CLERK'
    59	  const isAttendanceClerk = role === 'ATTENDANCE_CLERK'
    60	
    61	  useEffect(() => {
    62	    const fetchApplications = async () => {
    63	      const data = await getOvertimeApplications(session?.user?.id, session?.user?.role)
    64	      setApplications(data)
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    if (session) {
    68	      fetchApplications()
    69	    }
    70	  }, [session])
    71	
    72	  const filteredApplications = useMemo(
    73	    () => applications.filter((application) => isWithinTimeRange(application.date, timeRange)),
    74	    [applications, timeRange]
    75	  )
    76	  const pagination = useMemo(
    77	    () => getPaginationState(filteredApplications.length, currentPage, pageSize),
    78	    [currentPage, filteredApplications.length, pageSize]
    79	  )
    80	  const paginatedApplications = useMemo(
    81	    () => filteredApplications.slice(pagination.startIndex, pagination.endIndex),
    82	    [filteredApplications, pagination.endIndex, pagination.startIndex]
    83	  )
    84	
    85	  useEffect(() => {
    86	    setCurrentPage(1)
    87	  }, [timeRange, pageSize])
    88	
    89	  useEffect(() => {
    90	    if (pagination.currentPage !== currentPage) {
    91	      setCurrentPage(pagination.currentPage)
    92	    }
    93	  }, [currentPage, pagination.currentPage])
    94	
    95	  const emptyColSpan = 7 + (showApplicant ? 1 : 0) + (showActions ? 1 : 0)
    96	
    97	  return (
    98	    <div className="space-y-6">
    99	      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
   100	        <div>
   101	          <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">加班申请管理</h1>
   102	          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
   103	            统一查看申请进度，并按时间范围快速筛选记录。
   104	          </p>
   105	        </div>
   106	        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
   107	          {canCreate && (
   108	            <Button asChild>
   109	              <Link href="/dashboard/overtime/new">新增加班申请</Link>
   110	            </Button>
   111	          )}
   112	          {isAttendanceClerk && (
   113	            <>
   114	              <Button asChild>
   115	                <Link href="/dashboard/overtime/import">批量导入</Link>
   116	              </Button>
   117	              <Button
   118	                onClick={() => {
   119	                  const blob = new Blob([generateOvertimeTemplate()], {
   120	                    type: 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet',
   121	                  })
   122	                  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
   123	                  const a = document.createElement('a')
   124	                  a.href = url
   125	                  a.download = '加班导入模板.xlsx'
   126	                  a.click()
   127	                  URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
   128	                }}
   129	              >
   130	                下载模板
   131	              </Button>
   132	            </>
   133	          )}
   134	        </div>
   135	      </div>
   136	
   137	      <Card className="border-white/70 bg-white/85 shadow-lg backdrop-blur">
   138	        <CardHeader className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
   139	          <div>
   140	            <CardTitle>申请记录</CardTitle>
   141	            <div className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">当前共 {filteredApplications.length} 条记录</div>
   142	          </div>
   143	          <div className="w-full sm:w-52">
   144	            <Select value={timeRange} onChange={(event) => setTimeRange(event.target.value as TimeRange)}>
   145	              {timeRangeOptions.map((option) => (
   146	                <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
   147	                  {option.label}
   148	                </option>
   149	              ))}
   150	            </Select>
   151	          </div>
   152	        </CardHeader>
   153	        <CardContent>
   154	          <Table>
   155	            <TableHeader>
   156	              <TableRow>
   157	                <TableHead>日期</TableHead>
   158	                {showApplicant && <TableHead>申请人</TableHead>}
   159	                <TableHead>时间段</TableHead>
   160	                <TableHead>时长</TableHead>
   161	                <TableHead>事由</TableHead>
   162	                <TableHead>类型</TableHead>
   163	                <TableHead>状态</TableHead>
   164	                <TableHead>备注</TableHead>
   165	                {showActions && <TableHead>操作</TableHead>}
   166	              </TableRow>
   167	            </TableHeader>
   168	            <TableBody>
   169	              {filteredApplications.length === 0 ? (
   170	                <TableRow>
   171	                  <TableCell colSpan={emptyColSpan} className="py-8 text-center text-slate-500">
   172	                    当前筛选条件下暂无加班记录
   173	                  </TableCell>
   174	                </TableRow>
   175	              ) : (
   176	                paginatedApplications.map((application) => (
   177	                  <TableRow key={application.id}>
   178	                    <TableCell>{new Date(application.date).toLocaleDateString('zh-CN')}</TableCell>
   179	                    {showApplicant && <TableCell>{application.userName}</TableCell>}
   180	                    <TableCell>
   181	                      {new Date(application.startTime).toLocaleTimeString('zh-CN', {
   182	                        hour: '2-digit',
   183	                        minute: '2-digit',
   184	                      })}{' '}
   185	                      -{' '}
   186	                      {new Date(application.endTime).toLocaleTimeString('zh-CN', {
   187	                        hour: '2-digit',
   188	                        minute: '2-digit',
   189	                      })}
   190	                    </TableCell>
   191	                    <TableCell>{application.hours} 小时</TableCell>
   192	                    <TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate">{application.reason}</TableCell>
   193	                    <TableCell>
   194	                      {application.type === 'WEEKEND' ? '周末' : application.type === 'HOLIDAY' ? '节假日' : '工作日'}
   195	                    </TableCell>
   196	                    <TableCell>
   197	                      <Badge variant={statusVariant[application.status]}>{statusMap[application.status]}</Badge>
   198	                    </TableCell>
   199	                    <TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate">{application.remark || '-'}</TableCell>
   200	                    {showActions && (
   201	                      <TableCell>
   202	                        {application.status === 'DRAFT' ? (
   203	                          <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline">
   204	                            <Link href={`/dashboard/overtime/${application.id}`}>编辑</Link>
   205	                          </Button>
   206	                        ) : application.status === 'PRE_APPROVED' ? (
   207	                          <Button asChild size="sm" variant="default">
   208	                            <Link href={`/dashboard/overtime/${application.id}/confirm`}>提交确认</Link>
   209	                          </Button>
   210	                        ) : isAttendanceClerk && application.status === 'COMPLETED' && application.approverId === null ? (
   211	                          <Button
   212	                            size="sm"
   213	                            variant="destructive"
   214	                            onClick={async () => {
   215	                              if (!confirm('确定删除这条导入的加班记录吗？')) return
   216	                              const result = await deleteOvertimeApplication(application.id)
   217	                              if (result.error) {
   218	                                alert(result.error)
   219	                              } else {
   220	                                alert(result.success)
   221	                                const data = await getOvertimeApplications(session?.user?.id, session?.user?.role)
   222	                                setApplications(data)
   223	                              }
   224	                            }}
   225	                          >
   226	                            删除
   227	                          </Button>
   228	                        ) : (
   229	                          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
   230	                            {application.status === 'PENDING' && '事前审批中'}
   231	                            {application.status === 'CONFIRM_PENDING' && '确认审批中'}
   232	                            {application.status === 'COMPLETED' && '已完成'}
   233	                          </span>
   234	                        )}
   235	                      </TableCell>
   236	                    )}
   237	                  </TableRow>
   238	                ))
   239	              )}
   240	            </TableBody>
   241	          </Table>
   242	        </CardContent>
   243	        <CardContent className="pt-0">
   244	          <PaginationControls
   245	            currentPage={pagination.currentPage}
   246	            itemLabel="条记录"
   247	            onPageChange={setCurrentPage}
   248	            onPageSizeChange={setPageSize}
   249	            pageSize={pageSize}
   250	            pageSizeOptions={PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS}
   251	            totalItems={filteredApplications.length}
   252	            totalPages={pagination.totalPages}
   253	          />
   254	        </CardContent>
   255	      </Card>
   256	    </div>
   257	  )
   258	}

     1	'use server'

     3	import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client'
     4	import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'

     6	import { isAttendanceClerk, requireAttendanceClerk, requireSessionUser } from '@/lib/action-auth'

     9	import { overtimeSchema } from '@/lib/validations'

    11	
    12	async function requireOvertimeOwnerOrAdmin(id: string) {
    13	  const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
    14	  const application = await prisma.overtimeApplication.findUnique({
    15	    where: { id },
    16	    select: {
    17	      id: true,
    18	      userId: true,
    19	      status: true,
    20	      approverId: true,
    21	    },
    22	  })
    23	
    24	  if (!application) {
    25	    return {
    26	      sessionUser,
    27	      application: null,
    28	    }
    29	  }
    30	
    31	  if (sessionUser.role !== 'ADMIN' && sessionUser.id !== application.userId) {
    32	    throw new Error('无权操作该加班申请')
    33	  }
    34	
    35	  return {
    36	    sessionUser,
    37	    application,
    38	  }
    39	}
    40	
    41	export async function createOvertimeApplication(formData: FormData) {
    42	  try {
    43	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
    44	    const validatedData = overtimeSchema.parse({
    45	      date: formData.get('date'),
    46	      startTime: formData.get('startTime'),
    47	      endTime: formData.get('endTime'),
    48	      type: formData.get('type'),
    49	      reason: formData.get('reason'),
    50	    })
    51	
    52	    const startDateTime = new Date(`${validatedData.date} ${validatedData.startTime}`)
    53	    const endDateTime = new Date(`${validatedData.date} ${validatedData.endTime}`)
    54	    const hours = calculateHours(startDateTime, endDateTime)
    55	
    56	    if (hours <= 0) {
    57	      return { error: '结束时间必须晚于开始时间' }
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    const action = formData.get('action') === 'submit' ? 'submit' : 'save'
    61	    const created = await prisma.overtimeApplication.create({
    62	      data: {
    63	        userId: sessionUser.id,
    64	        date: startDateTime,
    65	        startTime: startDateTime,
    66	        endTime: endDateTime,
    67	        hours,
    68	        type: validatedData.type,
    69	        reason: validatedData.reason,
    70	        status: action === 'submit' ? 'PENDING' : 'DRAFT',
    71	      },
    72	    })
    73	
    74	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/overtime')
    75	
    76	    return {
    77	      success: action === 'submit' ? '加班申请已提交' : '加班草稿已保存',
    78	      id: created.id,
    79	      status: created.status,
    80	    }
    81	  } catch (error) {
    82	    if (error instanceof Error) {
    83	      return { error: error.message }
    84	    }
    85	    return { error: '提交失败，请稍后重试' }
    86	  }
    87	}
    88	
    89	export async function deleteOvertimeApplication(id: string) {
    90	  try {
    91	    if (!id) {
    92	      return { error: '缺少加班申请 ID' }
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
    96	    const application = await prisma.overtimeApplication.findUnique({
    97	      where: { id },
    98	      select: {
    99	        id: true,
   100	        userId: true,
   101	        status: true,
   102	        approverId: true,
   103	      },
   104	    })
   105	
   106	    if (!application) {
   107	      return { error: '加班申请不存在' }
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    const isOwner = sessionUser.id === application.userId
   111	    const isAdmin = sessionUser.role === 'ADMIN'
   112	    const isClerkDeletingImport =
   113	      isAttendanceClerk(sessionUser.role) &&
   114	      application.status === 'COMPLETED' &&
   115	      application.approverId === null
   116	
   117	    if (!isOwner && !isAdmin && !isClerkDeletingImport) {
   118	      return { error: '无权操作该加班申请' }
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    if (application.status !== 'DRAFT' && !isAdmin && !isClerkDeletingImport) {
   122	      return { error: '只有草稿状态的加班申请可以删除' }
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    await prisma.$transaction([
   126	      prisma.approval.deleteMany({
   127	        where: {
   128	          applicationId: id,
   129	          applicationType: 'OVERTIME',
   130	        },
   131	      }),
   132	      prisma.overtimeApplication.delete({
   133	        where: { id },
   134	      }),
   135	    ])
   136	
   137	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/overtime')
   138	    return { success: '加班申请已删除' }
   139	  } catch (error) {
   140	    if (error instanceof Error) {
   141	      return { error: error.message }
   142	    }
   143	    return { error: '删除失败，请稍后重试' }
   144	  }
   145	}
   146	
   147	export async function updateOvertimeApplication(formData: FormData) {
   148	  try {
   149	    const id = formData.get('id')
   150	    if (typeof id !== 'string' || !id) {
   151	      return { error: '缺少加班申请 ID' }
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    const validatedData = overtimeSchema.parse({
   155	      date: formData.get('date'),
   156	      startTime: formData.get('startTime'),
   157	      endTime: formData.get('endTime'),
   158	      type: formData.get('type'),
   159	      reason: formData.get('reason'),
   160	    })
   161	
   162	    const { application } = await requireOvertimeOwnerOrAdmin(id)
   163	
   164	    if (!application) {
   165	      return { error: '加班申请不存在' }
   166	    }
   167	
   168	    if (application.status !== 'DRAFT') {
   169	      return { error: '只有草稿状态的加班申请可以修改' }
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    const startDateTime = new Date(`${validatedData.date} ${validatedData.startTime}`)
   173	    const endDateTime = new Date(`${validatedData.date} ${validatedData.endTime}`)
   174	    const hours = calculateHours(startDateTime, endDateTime)
   175	
   176	    if (hours <= 0) {
   177	      return { error: '结束时间必须晚于开始时间' }
   178	    }
   179	
   180	    const nextStatus = formData.get('action') === 'submit' ? 'PENDING' : 'DRAFT'
   181	
   182	    await prisma.$transaction([
   183	      prisma.approval.deleteMany({
   184	        where: {
   185	          applicationId: id,
   186	          applicationType: 'OVERTIME',
   187	        },
   188	      }),
   189	      prisma.overtimeApplication.update({
   190	        where: { id },
   191	        data: {
   192	          date: startDateTime,
   193	          startTime: startDateTime,
   194	          endTime: endDateTime,
   195	          hours,
   196	          type: validatedData.type,
   197	          reason: validatedData.reason,
   198	          status: nextStatus,
   199	          approverId: null,
   200	          approvedAt: null,
   201	          remark: null,
   202	        },
   203	      }),
   204	    ])
   205	
   206	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/overtime')
   207	    return {
   208	      success:
   209	        nextStatus === 'PENDING' ? '加班申请已提交，审批流程已重新开始' : '加班申请已保存为草稿',
   210	    }
   211	  } catch (error) {
   212	    if (error instanceof Error) {
   213	      return { error: error.message }
   214	    }
   215	    return { error: '更新失败，请稍后重试' }
   216	  }
   217	}
   218	
   219	export async function getOvertimeApplications(_userId?: string, _role?: string) {
   220	  try {
   221	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
   222	    const where: Prisma.OvertimeApplicationWhereInput =
   223	      sessionUser.role === 'EMPLOYEE'
   224	        ? {
   225	            userId: sessionUser.id,
   226	          }
   227	        : {}
   228	
   229	    const applications = await prisma.overtimeApplication.findMany({
   230	      where,
   231	      include: {
   232	        user: {
   233	          select: {
   234	            name: true,
   235	            username: true,
   236	          },
   237	        },
   238	      },
   239	      orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
   240	    })
   241	
   242	    return applications.map((application) => ({
   243	      ...application,
   244	      userName: application.user.name,
   245	    }))
   246	  } catch (error) {
   247	    console.error('获取加班申请列表失败:', error)
   248	    return []
   249	  }
   250	}
   251	
   252	export async function getOvertimeApplication(id: string) {
   253	  try {
   254	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
   255	    const application = await prisma.overtimeApplication.findUnique({
   256	      where: { id },
   257	      include: {
   258	        user: {
   259	          select: {
   260	            name: true,
   261	            username: true,
   262	          },
   263	        },
   264	      },
   265	    })
   266	
   267	    if (!application) {
   268	      return null
   269	    }
   270	
   271	    if (sessionUser.role === 'EMPLOYEE' && application.userId !== sessionUser.id) {
   272	      return null
   273	    }
   274	
   275	    return {
   276	      ...application,
   277	      userName: application.user.name,
   278	    }

   281	    return null
   282	  }
   283	}
   284	
   285	export async function getOvertimeStats(userId?: string, departmentId?: string, referenceMonth?: string) {
   286	  try {
   287	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
   288	    const now = new Date()
   289	    const [year, month] = referenceMonth
   290	      ? referenceMonth.split('-').map(Number)
   291	      : [now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1]
   292	    const firstDayOfMonth = new Date(year, month - 1, 1)
   293	    const lastDayOfMonth = new Date(year, month, 0, 23, 59, 59)
   294	
   295	    const where: Prisma.OvertimeApplicationWhereInput = {
   296	      status: {
   297	        in: ['COMPLETED'],
   298	      },
   299	      date: {
   300	        gte: firstDayOfMonth,
   301	        lte: lastDayOfMonth,
   302	      },
   303	    }
   304	
   305	    if (sessionUser.role === 'EMPLOYEE') {
   306	      where.userId = sessionUser.id
   307	    } else if (userId) {
   308	      where.userId = userId
   309	    }
   310	
   311	    if (departmentId && sessionUser.role !== 'EMPLOYEE') {
   312	      where.user = {
   313	        departmentId,
   314	      }
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    const result = await prisma.overtimeApplication.aggregate({
   318	      where,
   319	      _sum: {
   320	        actualHours: true,
   321	      },
   322	    })
   323	
   324	    return result._sum.actualHours || 0
   325	  } catch (error) {
   326	    console.error('获取加班统计失败:', error)
   327	    return 0
   328	  }
   329	}

   331	// 提交加班确认（申请人填写实际加班时间后提交）
   332	export async function submitOvertimeConfirmation(formData: FormData) {
   333	  try {
   334	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
   335	    const id = formData.get('id') as string
   336	
   337	    if (!id) {
   338	      return { error: '缺少加班申请 ID' }
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    const application = await prisma.overtimeApplication.findUnique({
   342	      where: { id },
   343	      select: {
   344	        id: true,
   345	        userId: true,
   346	        status: true,
   347	      },
   348	    })
   349	
   350	    if (!application) {
   351	      return { error: '加班申请不存在' }

   353	
   354	    if (application.userId !== sessionUser.id) {
   355	      return { error: '无权操作该加班申请' }
   356	    }
   357	
   358	    if (application.status !== 'PRE_APPROVED') {
   359	      return { error: '只有事前审批通过的加班申请可以提交确认' }
   360	    }
   361	
   362	    const date = formData.get('date') as string
   363	    const startTime = formData.get('startTime') as string
   364	    const endTime = formData.get('endTime') as string
   365	
   366	    if (!date || !startTime || !endTime) {
   367	      return { error: '请填写完整的实际加班时间' }
   368	    }
   369	
   370	    const actualStartTime = new Date(`${date} ${startTime}`)
   371	    const actualEndTime = new Date(`${date} ${endTime}`)
   372	    const actualHours = calculateHours(actualStartTime, actualEndTime)
   373	
   374	    if (actualHours <= 0) {
   375	      return { error: '结束时间必须晚于开始时间' }
   376	    }
   377	
   378	    await prisma.overtimeApplication.update({
   379	      where: { id },
   380	      data: {
   381	        actualStartTime,
   382	        actualEndTime,
   383	        actualHours,
   384	        status: 'CONFIRM_PENDING',
   385	        currentPhase: 'CONFIRM',
   386	      },
   387	    })
   388	
   389	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/overtime')
   390	    return { success: '加班确认已提交，进入确认审批流程' }
   391	  } catch (error) {
   392	    if (error instanceof Error) {
   393	      return { error: error.message }
   394	    }
   395	    return { error: '提交确认失败，请稍后重试' }
   396	  }
   397	}
   398	
   399	
   400	// ===== 批量导入加班 =====
   401	
   402	export async function batchImportOvertime(rows: ParsedOvertimeRow[]) {
   403	  try {
   404	    await requireAttendanceClerk()
   405	
   406	    if (!rows || rows.length === 0) {
   407	      return { error: '没有数据需要导入' }
   408	    }
   409	
   410	    const usernames = Array.from(new Set(rows.map((r) => r.username)))
   411	    const users = await prisma.user.findMany({
   412	      where: {
   413	        username: {
   414	          in: usernames,
   415	        },
   416	      },
   417	      select: {
   418	        id: true,
   419	        username: true,
   420	        name: true,
   421	      },
   422	    })
   423	
   424	    const userMap = new Map(users.map((u) => [u.username, u]))
   425	    const errors: Array<{ rowIndex: number; message: string }> = []
   426	
   427	    for (const row of rows) {
   428	      const user = userMap.get(row.username)
   429	      if (!user) {
   430	        errors.push({ rowIndex: row.rowIndex, message: `用户 "${row.username}" 不存在` })
   431	      } else if (user.name !== row.name && row.name) {
   432	        errors.push({ rowIndex: row.rowIndex, message: `用户 "${row.username}" 的姓名不匹配（系统中为 ${user.name}）` })
   433	      }
   434	    }
   435	
   436	    if (errors.length > 0) {
   437	      return { error: '数据校验失败', errors }
   438	    }
   439	
   440	    const now = new Date()
   441	
   442	    await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
   443	      for (const row of rows) {
   444	        const user = userMap.get(row.username)!
   445	        const startDateTime = new Date(`${row.date} ${row.startTime}`)
   446	        const endDateTime = new Date(`${row.date} ${row.endTime}`)
   447	        const hours = calculateHours(startDateTime, endDateTime)
   448	
   449	        if (hours <= 0) {
   450	          throw new Error(`第 ${row.rowIndex} 行：结束时间必须晚于开始时间`)
   451	        }
   452	
   453	        await tx.overtimeApplication.create({
   454	          data: {
   455	            userId: user.id,
   456	            date: startDateTime,
   457	            startTime: startDateTime,
   458	            endTime: endDateTime,
   459	            hours,
   460	            actualStartTime: startDateTime,
   461	            actualEndTime: endDateTime,
   462	            actualHours: hours,
   463	            type: row.type,
   464	            reason: row.reason,
   465	            status: 'COMPLETED',
   466	            currentPhase: 'CONFIRM',
   467	            approverId: null,
   468	            approvedAt: now,
   469	          },
   470	        })
   471	      }
   472	    })
   473	
   474	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/overtime')
   475	    return { success: `成功导入 ${rows.length} 条加班记录` }
   476	  } catch (error) {
   477	    if (error instanceof Error) {
   478	      return { error: error.message }
   479	    }
   480	    return { error: '导入失败，请稍后重试' }
   481	  }
   482	}


</details>

    10	async function requirePerformanceOwnerOrManager(id: string) {
    11	  const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
    12	  const review = await prisma.performanceReview.findUnique({
    13	    where: { id },
    14	    select: {
    15	      id: true,
    16	      userId: true,
    17	    },
    18	  })
    19	
    20	  if (!review) {
    21	    return {
    22	      sessionUser,
    23	      review: null,
    24	    }
    25	  }
    26	
    27	  if (sessionUser.role === 'EMPLOYEE' && sessionUser.id !== review.userId) {
    28	    throw new Error('无权操作该绩效记录')
    29	  }
    30	
    31	  return {
    32	    sessionUser,
    33	    review,
    34	  }
    35	}
    36	
    37	function buildScorePayload(formData: FormData) {
    38	  return performanceSchema.parse({
    39	    period: formData.get('period'),
    40	    quality: Number(formData.get('quality')),
    41	    efficiency: Number(formData.get('efficiency')),
    42	    attitude: Number(formData.get('attitude')),
    43	    skill: Number(formData.get('skill')),
    44	    teamwork: Number(formData.get('teamwork')),
    45	    comment: formData.get('comment'),
    46	    selfComment: formData.get('selfComment'),
    47	  })
    48	}
    49	
    50	function calculateTotalScore(validatedData: ReturnType<typeof buildScorePayload>) {
    51	  return (
    52	    validatedData.quality +
    53	    validatedData.efficiency +
    54	    validatedData.attitude +
    55	    validatedData.skill +
    56	    validatedData.teamwork
    57	  ) / 5
    58	}
    59	
    60	export async function createPerformanceReview(formData: FormData) {
    61	  try {
    62	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
    63	    const validatedData = buildScorePayload(formData)
    64	    const totalScore = calculateTotalScore(validatedData)
    65	
    66	    const existing = await prisma.performanceReview.findFirst({
    67	      where: {
    68	        userId: sessionUser.id,
    69	        period: validatedData.period,
    70	      },
    71	    })
    72	
    73	    if (existing) {
    74	      return { error: '该期间的绩效已存在' }
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    const action = formData.get('action') === 'submit' ? 'submit' : 'save'
    78	    const created = await prisma.performanceReview.create({
    79	      data: {
    80	        userId: sessionUser.id,
    81	        period: validatedData.period,
    82	        quality: validatedData.quality,
    83	        efficiency: validatedData.efficiency,
    84	        attitude: validatedData.attitude,
    85	        skill: validatedData.skill,
    86	        teamwork: validatedData.teamwork,
    87	        totalScore,
    88	        comment: validatedData.comment,
    89	        selfComment: validatedData.selfComment,
    90	        reviewerId: sessionUser.id,
    91	        status: action === 'submit' ? 'PENDING' : 'DRAFT',
    92	      },
    93	    })
    94	
    95	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/performance')
    96	    return {
    97	      success: action === 'submit' ? '绩效记录已提交' : '绩效草稿已保存',
    98	      id: created.id,
    99	      status: created.status,
   100	    }
   101	  } catch (error) {
   102	    if (error instanceof Error) {
   103	      return { error: error.message }
   104	    }
   105	    return { error: '提交失败，请稍后重试' }
   106	  }
   107	}
   108	
   109	export async function updatePerformanceReview(formData: FormData) {
   110	  try {
   111	    const id = formData.get('id')
   112	    if (typeof id !== 'string' || !id) {
   113	      return { error: '缺少绩效记录 ID' }
   114	    }
   115	
   116	    const validatedData = buildScorePayload(formData)
   117	    const totalScore = calculateTotalScore(validatedData)
   118	    const { review } = await requirePerformanceOwnerOrManager(id)
   119

   123

   136	        status: formData.get('action') === 'submit' ? 'PENDING' : undefined,
   137	      },
   138	    })
   139	
   140	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/performance')
   141	    return {
   142	      success: formData.get('action') === 'submit' ? '绩效记录已提交' : '绩效记录已保存',
   143	    }
   144	  } catch (error) {
   145	    if (error instanceof Error) {

   147	    }

   151	
   152	export async function deletePerformanceReview(id: string) {
   153	  try {
   154	    if (!id) {
   155	      return { error: '缺少绩效记录 ID' }
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    const { review } = await requirePerformanceOwnerOrManager(id)
   159

   168	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/performance')

   177	
   178	export async function getPerformanceReviews(_userId?: string, _role?: string) {
   179	  try {
   180	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
   181	    const where: Prisma.PerformanceReviewWhereInput =
   182	      sessionUser.role === 'EMPLOYEE'
   183	        ? {
   184	            userId: sessionUser.id,

   187	
   188	    const reviews = await prisma.performanceReview.findMany({

   192	          select: {
   193	            name: true,
   194	            username: true,
   195	          },
   196	        },
   197	        reviewer: {
   198	          select: {
   199	            name: true,
   200	          },
   201	        },
   202	      },
   203	      orderBy: { period: 'desc' },
   204	    })
   205	
   206	    return reviews.map((review) => ({
   207	      ...review,
   208	      userName: review.user.name,
   209	      reviewerName: review.reviewer?.name || null,
   210	    }))
   211	  } catch (error) {
   212	    console.error('获取绩效记录失败:', error)
   213	    return []
   214	  }
   215	}
   216	
   217	export async function getPerformanceReview(id: string) {
   218	  try {
   219	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
   220	    const review = await prisma.performanceReview.findUnique({
   221	      where: { id },
   222	    })
   223	
   224	    if (!review) {
   225	      return null
   226	    }
   227	
   228	    if (sessionUser.role === 'EMPLOYEE' && sessionUser.id !== review.userId) {
   229	      return null

    47	export function OtherClientPage({
    48	  initialApplications,
    49	  viewerId,
    50	  viewerRole,
    51	}: OtherClientPageProps) {
    52	  const [applications, setApplications] = useState(initialApplications)
    53	  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
    54	  const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE)
    55	
    56	  const canCreate = Boolean(viewerId)
    57	  const canEdit = viewerRole === 'EMPLOYEE'
    58	
    59	  useEffect(() => {
    60	    setApplications(initialApplications)
    61	  }, [initialApplications])
    62

    64	    () => getPaginationState(applications.length, page, pageSize),
    65	    [applications.length, page, pageSize]
    66	  )
    67	
    68	  const paginatedApplications = useMemo(
    69	    () => applications.slice(pagination.startIndex, pagination.endIndex),
    70	    [applications, pagination.endIndex, pagination.startIndex]
    71	  )
    72

    76	
    77	  useEffect(() => {
    78	    if (pagination.currentPage !== page) {
    79	      setPage(pagination.currentPage)
    80	    }
    81	  }, [page, pagination.currentPage])
    82	
    83	  return (
    84	    <div className="space-y-6">
    85	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
    86	        <div>
    87	          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">其他事项申请</h1>
    88	          <p className="mt-1 text-gray-600">提交离职交接、履历更新、党员信息更新等申请。</p>
    89	        </div>
    90	        {canCreate && (
    91	          <Button asChild>
    92	            <Link href="/dashboard/other/new">新增申请</Link>
    93	          </Button>
    94	        )}
    95	      </div>
    96	
    97	      <Card>
    98	        <CardHeader>
    99	          <CardTitle>申请记录</CardTitle>
   100	        </CardHeader>
   101	        <CardContent>
   102	          <Table>
   103	            <TableHeader>
   104	              <TableRow>
   105	                <TableHead>事项类型</TableHead>
   106	                <TableHead>申请标题</TableHead>
   107	                {viewerRole !== 'EMPLOYEE' && <TableHead>申请人</TableHead>}
   108	                <TableHead>申请内容</TableHead>
   109	                <TableHead>状态</TableHead>
   110	                <TableHead>备注</TableHead>
   111	                <TableHead>创建时间</TableHead>
   112	                {canEdit && <TableHead>操作</TableHead>}
   113	              </TableRow>
   114	            </TableHeader>
   115	            <TableBody>
   116	              {applications.length === 0 ? (
   117	                <TableRow>
   118	                  <TableCell colSpan={viewerRole === 'EMPLOYEE' ? 7 : 8} className="py-8 text-center text-gray-500">
   119	                    暂无申请记录
   120	                  </TableCell>
   121	                </TableRow>
   122	              ) : (
   123	                paginatedApplications.map((application) => (
   124	                  <OtherApplicationRow
   125	                    key={application.id}
   126	                    application={application}
   127	                    canEdit={canEdit}
   128	                    showApplicant={viewerRole !== 'EMPLOYEE'}
   129	                  />
   130	                ))
   131	              )}
   132	            </TableBody>
   133	          </Table>
   134	        </CardContent>
   135	        <CardContent className="pt-0">
   136	          <PaginationControls
   137	            currentPage={pagination.currentPage}
   138	            itemLabel="条申请记录"
   139	            onPageChange={setPage}
   140	            onPageSizeChange={setPageSize}
   141	            pageSize={pageSize}
   142	            pageSizeOptions={PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS}
   143	            totalItems={applications.length}
   144	            totalPages={pagination.totalPages}
   145	          />
   146	        </CardContent>
   147	      </Card>
   148	    </div>
   149	  )
   150	}
   151	
   152	function OtherApplicationRow({
   153	  application,
   154	  canEdit,
   155	  showApplicant,
   156	}: {
   157	  application: OtherApplicationItem
   158	  canEdit: boolean
   159	  showApplicant: boolean
   160	}) {
   161	  return (
   162	    <TableRow>
   163	      <TableCell>
   164	        <Badge variant="outline">{typeMap[application.type] || application.type}</Badge>
   165	      </TableCell>
   166	      <TableCell className="font-medium">{application.title}</TableCell>
   167	      {showApplicant && <TableCell>{application.userName}</TableCell>}
   168	      <TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate">{application.content}</TableCell>
   169	      <TableCell>
   170	        <Badge variant={statusVariant[application.status] || 'default'}>
   171	          {statusMap[application.status] || application.status}
   172	        </Badge>
   173	      </TableCell>
   174	      <TableCell className="max-w-xs truncate">{application.remark || '-'}</TableCell>
   175	      <TableCell>{formatDateTime(new Date(application.createdAt))}</TableCell>
   176	      {canEdit && (
   177	        <TableCell>
   178	          {application.status === 'DRAFT' ? (
   179	            <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm">
   180	              <Link href={`/dashboard/other/${application.id}`}>编辑</Link>
   181	            </Button>
   182	          ) : (
   183	            <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">审批中 / 已完成</span>
   184	          )}
   185	        </TableCell>
   186	      )}
   187	    </TableRow>
   188	  )
   189	}

    11	export async function getCompensatoryInfo(userId: string) {
    12	  try {
    13	    await requireSelfOrAdmin(userId)
    14	
    15	    const leaveBalance = await ensureLeaveBalance(userId)
    16	    const settledOvertime = await prisma.overtimeSettlement.findMany({
    17	      where: { userId },
    18	      select: { hours: true },
    19	    })
    20	    const settledOvertimeHours = settledOvertime.reduce((sum, settlement) => sum + settlement.hours, 0)
    21	
    22	    return {
    23	      totalCompensatory: leaveBalance.compensatory || 0,
    24	      availableCompensatory: (leaveBalance.compensatory || 0) - (leaveBalance.usedCompensatory || 0),
    25	      usedCompensatory: leaveBalance.usedCompensatory || 0,
    26	      settledOvertimeHours,
    27	    }
    28	  } catch (error) {
    29	    console.error('获取调休信息失败:', error)
    30	    return {
    31	      totalCompensatory: 0,
    32	      availableCompensatory: 0,
    33	      usedCompensatory: 0,
    34	      settledOvertimeHours: 0,
    35	    }
    36	  }
    37	}

    39	export async function useCompensatory(formData: FormData) {
    40	  try {
    41	    const sessionUser = await requireSessionUser()
    42	    const validatedData = compensatoryUseSchema.parse({
    43	      hours: formData.get('hours'),
    44	      startDate: formData.get('startDate'),
    45	      reason: formData.get('reason'),
    46	    })
    47	
    48	    const hoursToUse = Number(validatedData.hours)
    49	    const leaveBalance = await ensureLeaveBalance(sessionUser.id)
    50	    const availableCompensatory = leaveBalance.compensatory - leaveBalance.usedCompensatory
    51	
    52	    if (availableCompensatory < hoursToUse) {
    53	      return { error: `调休余额不足，当前可用 ${availableCompensatory} 小时` }
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    const startDate = new Date(validatedData.startDate)
    57	    const isFullDay = hoursToUse === SALARY_CONSTANTS.FULL_DAY_HOURS
    58	
    59	    const [leaveApplication] = await prisma.$transaction([
    60	      prisma.leaveApplication.create({
    61	        data: {
    62	          userId: sessionUser.id,
    63	          type: 'PERSONAL',
    64	          startDate,
    65	          endDate: startDate,
    66	          days: isFullDay ? 1 : 0.5,
    67	          reason: `[调休] ${validatedData.reason}`,
    68	          status: 'PENDING',
    69	        },
    70	      }),
    71	      prisma.leaveBalance.update({
    72	        where: { userId: sessionUser.id },
    73	        data: {
    74	          usedCompensatory: {
    75	            increment: hoursToUse,
    76	          },
    77	        },
    78	      }),
    79	    ])
    80	
    81	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/compensatory')
    82	    revalidatePath('/dashboard/leave')
    83	    return {
    84	      success: '调休申请已提交，等待审批',
    85	      leaveApplicationId: leaveApplication.id,
    86	    }
    87	  } catch (error) {
    88	    if (error instanceof Error) {
    89	      return { error: error.message }
    90	    }
    91	    return { error: '申请失败，请稍后重试' }
    92	  }
    93	}

     1	import { prisma } from '@/lib/prisma'
     2	import { calculateAnnualLeaveEntitlement } from '@/lib/seniority'
     3	
     4	const DEFAULT_ANNUAL_LEAVE_DAYS = 5
     5	
     6	type LeaveBalanceDbClient = Pick<typeof prisma, 'user' | 'leaveBalance'>
     7	
     8	async function getAnnualLeaveEntitlement(client: LeaveBalanceDbClient, userId: string) {
     9	  const user = await client.user.findUnique({
    10	    where: { id: userId },
    11	    select: {
    12	      firstWorkDate: true,
    13	    },
    14	  })
    15	
    16	  if (!user) {
    17	    return DEFAULT_ANNUAL_LEAVE_DAYS
    18	  }
    19	
    20	  return calculateAnnualLeaveEntitlement(user.firstWorkDate)
    21	}
    22	
    23	export async function ensureLeaveBalance(userId: string, client: LeaveBalanceDbClient = prisma) {
    24	  const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear()
    25	  const annualEntitlement = await getAnnualLeaveEntitlement(client, userId)
    26	
    27	  const existingBalance = await client.leaveBalance.findUnique({
    28	    where: { userId },
    29	  })
    30	
    31	  if (!existingBalance) {
    32	    return client.leaveBalance.create({
    33	      data: {
    34	        userId,
    35	        year: currentYear,
    36	        annual: annualEntitlement,
    37	        annualEntitlement,
    38	        compensatory: 0,
    39	        usedCompensatory: 0,
    40	      },
    41	    })
    42	  }
    43	
    44	  if (existingBalance.year !== currentYear) {
    45	    return client.leaveBalance.update({
    46	      where: { userId },
    47	      data: {
    48	        year: currentYear,
    49	        annual: annualEntitlement,
    50	        annualEntitlement,
    51	      },
    52	    })
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  const currentEntitlement = existingBalance.annualEntitlement ?? DEFAULT_ANNUAL_LEAVE_DAYS
    56	  if (currentEntitlement !== annualEntitlement) {
    57	    const nextAnnual = Math.max(existingBalance.annual + annualEntitlement - currentEntitlement, 0)
    58	
    59	    return client.leaveBalance.update({
    60	      where: { userId },
    61	      data: {
    62	        annual: nextAnnual,
    63	        annualEntitlement,
    64	      },
    65	    })
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  return existingBalance
    69	}


</details>

    22	function findBestMatch(pathname: string, hrefs: string[]): string | null {
    23	  const exact = hrefs.find((h) => h === pathname)
    24	  if (exact) return exact
    25	
    26	  const candidates = hrefs
    27	    .filter((h) => h !== '/dashboard' && pathname.startsWith(`${h}/`))

    29	
    30	  return candidates[0] || null
    31	}

    35	  return best === href

<details><summary>Thinking</summary>

现在我已经充分了解了代码库的结构和权限逻辑。让我总结一下用户描述的问题：

1. **请假管理页面无法看到个人的年假剩余时长和调休记录时长**
   - 当前 `leave-client-page.tsx` 中确实显示了 `balances`（年假、病假、事假、调休可用），但这只在 `canCreate && balances` 条件下显示。
   - `canCreate = Boolean(viewerId)`，也就是说只要有 viewerId 就会显示。
   - 问题可能是 `ensureLeaveBalance` 在某些情况下返回 null，或者某些角色（如 ATTENDANCE_CLERK）看不到。
   - 看 sidebar：`if (!isAttendanceClerk)` 才会显示"我的申请"分组，考勤员走的是"考勤管理"分组，但两个分组都有 `/dashboard/leave` 链接。考勤员打开 `/dashboard/leave` 时，`canCreate = true` 但 `viewerRole === 'ATTENDANCE_CLERK'`，`canEdit = true`。
   - 等等，用户说的是"无法看到个人的年假剩余时长和调休记录时长"，这可能是 EMPLOYEE 角色的问题。
   - 让我仔细看 `leave-client-page.tsx` 第 155 行：`{balances && canCreate && (`，这里 balances 可能是 null。在 page.tsx 中，`ensureLeaveBalance(viewerId)` 被 try-catch 包裹，如果出错则设为 null。
   - 但我需要确认：用户的问题究竟是什么？当前逻辑是 EMPLOYEE 只能看到自己的请假申请，MANAGER/ADMIN/ATTENDANCE_CLERK 可以看到所有人的。而用户说"加班申请、绩效管理、其他事项、请假管理只能看到自己的，而查询统计，admin系统角色可以看到所有人的"。

2. **加班申请、绩效管理、其他事项、请假管理只能看到自己的**
   - 当前代码中：
     - `getLeaveApplications`: `sessionUser.role === 'EMPLOYEE' ? { userId: sessionUser.id } : {}` — EMPLOYEE 只能看自己的，其他角色（MANAGER, ADMIN, ATTENDANCE_CLERK）可以看所有人的。
     - `getOvertimeApplications`: 同上。
     - `getPerformanceReviews`: 同上。
     - `getOtherApplications`: 同上。
   - 用户说这些只能看到自己的，而查询统计中 admin 可以看到所有人的。
   - 用户的意思是：希望 ADMIN 也能在这些模块看到所有人的数据，而不仅仅是查询统计中。
   - 或者用户可能是在描述当前的现象：这些模块只能看到自己的，但查询统计 admin 可以看到所有人的。用户希望改变这个现状？

等等，让我重新理解用户的描述：
"请假管理页面无法看到个人的年假剩余时长和调休记录时长，且加班申请、绩效管理、其他事项、请假管理只能看到自己的，而查询统计，admin系统角色可以看到所有人的"

这看起来是在描述当前系统的**问题/需求**：
1. 请假管理页面看不到年假剩余和调休记录（可能是 bug）
2. 加班申请、绩效管理、其他事项、请假管理只能看到自己的 —— 这是当前的限制，用户希望 ADMIN 也能看到所有人的（像查询统计一样）

但我不确定用户是希望：
- ADMIN 在这些页面也能看到所有人的数据？
- 还是只有查询统计中 ADMIN 才能看到所有人的，其他页面保持只能看到自己的？

让我看看查询统计的权限：`requireManagerUser()` — 需要 ADMIN 或 MANAGER。

再看 sidebar：
- 考勤员（ATTENDANCE_CLERK）没有"我的申请"分组，但有"考勤管理"分组包含加班导入和请假导入，他们访问这些页面时能看到所有数据（因为 role !== EMPLOYEE）。
- MANAGER 有"审批中心"分组，包含"待办审批"和"查询统计"。
- ADMIN 有"系统管理"分组。

用户说"admin系统角色可以看到所有人的"，这确实如此，因为查询统计要求 `ADMIN || MANAGER`。

但用户的核心问题似乎是：
1. **请假管理页面看不到年假剩余和调休记录** —— 可能是一个 bug
2. **ADMIN 在加班申请、绩效管理、其他事项、请假管理页面也应该能看到所有人的数据** —— 这是一个功能需求

等等，让我再想想。用户说"且加班申请、绩效管理、其他事项、请假管理只能看到自己的，而查询统计，admin系统角色可以看到所有人的"。这句话的语气和上下文暗示用户认为这是一个**问题**——即这些地方 admin 看不到所有人的，只有查询统计可以。

但也有可能是用户只是在陈述现状，没有明确说要改。

不过，用户说"请假管理页面无法看到个人的年假剩余时长和调休记录时长"，这明确是一个 bug 报告。

让我分析一下为什么看不到年假剩余时长和调休记录：
- 在 `LeaveClientPage` 中，`balances` 条件是 `{balances && canCreate && (...)}`
- `canCreate = Boolean(viewerId)` — 只要有 viewerId 就是 true
- 所以只要 `balances` 不是 null 就会显示
- `balances` 来自 `initialBalances`，由 `ensureLeaveBalance(viewerId)` 提供
- `ensureLeaveBalance` 会查找或创建 `LeaveBalance` 记录
- 如果 `viewerId` 存在且数据库正常，应该能获取到

但等等，`leave-client-page.tsx` 第 56 行：`const [balances, setBalances] = useState(initialBalances)`。之后在 useEffect 中有一个刷新逻辑吗？没有直接刷新的。但是 `getLeaveApplications()` 被调用后没有更新 balances。

不过这不是用户说的问题。用户说的是"无法看到"，可能是：
1. 年假剩余时长显示的是 `balances.annual`，但 `annualEntitlement`（年假 entitlement/ entitlement）没有显示
2. 或者年假剩余时长指的是 `annualEntitlement - used` 之类的？

实际上，代码中显示：
- 年假：`balances.annual` 天
- 调休可用：`(balances.compensatory || 0) - (balances.usedCompensatory || 0)` 小时

用户说"无法看到个人的年假剩余时长和调休记录时长"。这里的"剩余时长"可能指的是年假还剩下多少天（已经显示了），也可能指的是年假 entitlement（总年假）没有显示。但看代码，年假显示的是 `balances.annual`，这应该是剩余天数。

等等，`leave-balance.ts` 中的逻辑：
- 创建时 `annual: annualEntitlement`
- 但没有在请假审批通过后扣减年假的逻辑？让我看看...

在 `batchImportLeave` 中，导入时会扣减余额。在 `createLeaveApplication` 中，只是检查余额，但提交后并没有扣减余额！这可能就是问题所在——余额没有在审批通过后更新。

让我看看审批流程中是否有扣减余额的逻辑。
